right in combinations(sorted(set(group)), 2):\n            if not are_legal_form_variants(left, right):\n                continue\n            ordered = (left, right) if left < right else (right, left)\n            if ordered in seen_variant_pairs:\n                continue\n            seen_variant_pairs.add(ordered)\n            if \"legal_form_name_variant\" not in declared:\n                continue\n            tv.assert_tuple(\n                \"legal_form_name_variant\",\n                {\"name_a\": ordered[0], \"name_b\": ordered[1]},\n                grounding=[\n                    Grounding(\n                        GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                        \"mechanical:legal_form\",\n                        f\"stem({left!r})==stem({right!r})\",\n                    )\n                ],\n            )\n            candidate_count += 1\n\n    referent_count = tv.query(\"SELECT count(*) AS n FROM _tv_referents\")[0][\"n\"]\n\n    report = {\n        \"referents\":
